Verbatim User-Agent header, truncated to 2048 characters; NULL when the header is absent.\",\n \"name\": \"user_agent\",\n \"type\": \"TEXT\"\n }\n ],\n \"indexes\": [\n \"Index on clicks(link_id, clicked_at DESC, id DESC): serves both the paginated newest-first clicks list and the COUNT(*) for total_clicks in the stats endpoint. Without it every stats call scans the whole clicks table, which grows unbounded with traffic.\"\n ],\n \"name\": \"clicks\",\n \"purpose\": \"Append-only redirect log. Deliberately has no column capable of holding a client IP address.\"\n }\n ],\n \"decisions\": [\n {\n \"choice\": \"SQLite single file opened with PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL, PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL, PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON, busy_timeout=5000ms; schema created idempotently at application startup via CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS.\",\n \"decision\": \"Storage engine and mode\",\n \"rationale\": \"Deployment is single node and the redirect path is one unique-index lookup plus one small insert; WAL lets the click insert proceed without blocking concurrent redirect reads, so a burst of visitors does not serialize behind each other's writes. busy_timeout turns a transient lock into a slightly slower redirect instead of a 500.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"Postgres or Redis: an external datastore is forbidden by the constraints and adds an operational dependency for a workload of one row read per click.\",\n \"Default rollback journal: writers block readers, so a click insert would stall concurrent redirects.\",\n \"Migration framework such as Alembic: for two tables created once at startup it is more machinery than the schema is worth.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"Expired links return 410 Gone with {\\\"error\\\":{\\\"code\\\":\\\"link_expired\\\",...}}; unknown or malformed codes return 404 with {\\\"error\\\":{\\\"code\\\":\\\"not_found\\\",...}}. The two are deliberately distinguishable.\",\n \"decision\": \"What an expired code returns, and whether it is distinguishable from a code that never existed\",\n \"rationale\": \"A user who clicks a shared link needs to know the link was real and has lapsed (so they can ask for a fresh one) rather than that they mistyped. Codes are 62^7 random, so the existence oracle leaks nothing an attacker could enumerate; the acceptance criteria also pin 410 for expired and 404 for unknown. Expired links are retained rather than deleted so their analytics remain readable at /stats.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"Returning 404 for both: hides the difference between a lapsed link and a typo and would fail the pinned 410 criterion.\",\n \"Deleting rows at expiry: destroys the accumulated click history that the stats endpoint must still serve for expired links.\",\n \"Returning 410 with a Location header: some clients would follow it, defeating revocation.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"Synchronously, inside the request handler, before the 307 response is returned: read the link, evaluate expiry, INSERT the click row and commit, then send the response.\",\n \"decision\": \"Where the click analytics write happens relative to the redirect response\",\n \"rationale\": \"The criteria require exactly one click row per successful redirect and total_clicks to equal successful redirects; a background task or buffered flush makes the stats endpoint return a stale count immediately after a click, which a user watching their dashboard would see as a lost click. The insert is a single append into a WAL-mode file, on the order of tens of microseconds, which is invisible next to the visitor's DNS+TCP to the destination.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"FastAPI BackgroundTasks or a queue flusher: a crash or a race between the redirect and a stats read loses or delays clicks, and tests asserting exactly one row become timing-dependent.\",\n \"Writing before expiry evaluation: would record clicks for expired links, violating the 'no click row on 410' criterion.\",\n \"Fire-and-forget logging to a file parsed later: adds a second source of truth and a file that could accumulate request metadata.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"An ASGI middleware that runs before routing/body parsing and applies only when method == POST and path == /api/links. State is an in-process dict mapping a per-boot-salted SHA-256 of the client address to a deque of monotonic timestamps, pruned to the LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS rolling window; nothing is written to SQLite, disk, or logs. Client address is request.client.host, or the first X-Forwarded-For entry when LINKS_TRUST_FORWARDED_FOR is true. Retry-After = ceil(seconds until the oldest in-window timestamp ages out), minimum 1.\",\n \"decision\": \"Layer and key for rate limiting\",\n \"rationale\": \"Enforcing it before body parsing means a flood of oversized bodies is rejected cheaply, and the 429 arrives without touching the database, satisfying 'writes no row'. Hashing with a per-boot random salt means a memory dump or a crash report never yields a visitor IP, and the state vanishes on restart, satisfying 'no persisted client IP'. Because the limiter is keyed per address, a second client in the same window still gets 201.\",\n \"rejected\": [\n \"A SQLite-backed limiter table: would persist client addresses, directly violating the privacy constraint.\",\n \"Per-route dependency after body validation: a large invalid body would still be parsed before the limit applied, and 429 ordering versus 422 would become ambiguous.\",\n \"Global (non-keyed) limiting: one noisy client would lock out everyone.\",\n \"Redis token bucket: forbidden external datastore; unnecessary on a single node.\"\n ]\n },\n {\n \"choice\": \"Pure parsing plus name resolution: urlsplit the URL, require scheme in {http, https} and a non-empty host; if the host parses as an IP literal (via ipaddress, after unwrapping brackets, IPv4-mapped ::ffff:a.b.c.d and IPv4-compatible forms, and after normalizing decimal/octal/hex IPv4 literals through the int/ipaddress constructors) check it directly; otherwise call socket.getaddrinfo for AF_UNSPEC and check every returned address. Reject if any address is loopback, private, link-local (including 169.254.169.254), unique-local, CGNAT 100.64.0.0/10, multicast, unspecified, or otherwise reserved. Unresolvable hosts are rejected with blocked_destination (fail closed). No HTTP client is ever constructed for a user-supplied URL, at creation or at redirect.\",\n \"decision\": \"How d\n\nACCEPTANCE CRITERIA\n- POST /api/links with body {\"url\": \"https://example.com/page?a=1\"} returns 201 and a JSON body containing code, short_url, url (echoed unchanged including query string), created_at and expires_at; a corresponding row exists in the links table.\n- POST /api/links with no expiry field stores expires_at equal to created_at plus 30 days (within 1 second tolerance) and returns that value in the response.\n- POST /api/links with an ISO-8601 expires_at in the future stores and returns exactly that instant, normalized to UTC.\n- POST /api/links with an expires_at that is in the past, equal to now, or not a valid ISO-8601 timestamp returns 400 with a JSON error body and writes no row.\n- POST /api/links with url using a scheme other than http or https (e.g. ftp://host/f, file:///etc/passwd, javascript:alert(1), data:text/html,x) returns 400 with error code unsupported_scheme and writes no row.\n- POST /api/links with a url whose host is a literal loopback address (127.0.0.1, 127.5.5.5, ::1, [::ffff:127.0.0.1]) returns 400 with error code blocked_destination and writes no row.\n- POST /api/links with a url whose host is a literal private or reserved address (10.0.0.1, 172.16.0.5, 192.168.1.1, 169.254.169.254, 100.64.0.1, 0.0.0.0, fd00::1, fe80::1) returns 400 with error code blocked_destination and writes no row.\n- POST /api/links with a url whose hostname resolves via DNS to a blocked address (test stubs the resolver to return 127.0.0.1) returns 400 with error code blocked_destination and writes no row.\n- POST /api/links with a hostname resolving to multiple addresses where at least one is blocked returns 400 and writes no row.\n- During creation and during redirect the service makes no outbound HTTP(S) request to the destination: a test that patches the HTTP client layer asserts zero calls while creation and redirect both succeed.\n- POST /api/links with a missing url field, a non-string url, an empty string, or a url longer than the configured maximum (2048 characters) returns 422 or 400 with a JSON error body and writes no row.\n- GET /{code} for an existing, unexpired link returns 307 with a Location header exactly equal to the stored URL and Cache-Control: no-store, and inserts exactly one click row.\n- The click row written by GET /{code} contains the request timestamp, the Referer header value (null when absent) and the User-Agent header value (null when absent), and the clicks table schema contains no column holding a client IP address.\n- GET /{code} for a code that does not exist returns 404 with a JSON error body and inserts no click row.\n- GET /{code} for a link whose expires_at is in the past returns 410 with a JSON error body, sends no Location header, and inserts no click row.\n- GET /api/links/{code}/stats for an existing link returns 200 with JSON containing code, url, created_at, expires_at, total_clicks and a clicks array whose entries each carry timestamp, referrer and user_agent; total_clicks equals the number of successful redirects served for that code.\n- GET /api/links/{code}/stats for an expired but existing link returns 200 with the accumulated analytics (not 410).\n- GET /api/links/{code}/stats for an unknown code returns 404 with a JSON error body.\n- GET /api/links/{code}/stats supports limit and offset query parameters on the clicks array with a default limit of 50 and a maximum of 500; limit=1 on a link with 3 clicks returns 1 click entry and total_clicks 3.\n- After the configured per-IP creation limit is reached (default 10 creations per 60-second rolling window), the next POST /api/links from the same client address returns 429 with a Retry-After header and writes no row, while a request from a different client address in the same window still returns 201.\n- Rate limiting state holds no persisted client IP: no table or file written by the service contains a client IP address after the rate limiter has been exercised.\n- GET /health returns 200 with {\"status\":\"ok\"} when the SQLite database answers a trivial query, and returns 503 with {\"status\":\"degraded\"} when the database is unavailable.\n- Generating 1000 codes yields 1000 distinct values, each matching ^[A-Za-z0-9]{7}$, produced by a cryptographically secure random source; codes created in sequence are not ordered or incrementable (a test asserts consecutive codes differ in more than the last character).\n- When code generation collides with an existing code, the service retries and still returns 201 with a unique code (test forces one collision via a seeded/stubbed generator).\n- GET /{code} where code does not match the code character set and length returns 404 rather than a server error.\n- All error responses across every endpoint are JSON objects with a stable shape {\"error\": {\"code\": <string>, \"message\": <string>}} and the documented HTTP status.\n\nCONSTRAINTS\n- Runtime: Python 3.11+ with FastAPI; all endpoints exchange JSON except the redirect response.\n- Storage: SQLite single file, schema created/migrated at application startup; no external datastore.\n- No authentication, user accounts, or API keys in this release; POST /api/links and the stats endpoint are publicly reachable.\n- Abuse control must be per-client-IP rate limiting on POST /api/links only.\n- Destination validation is a denylist: every public http/https URL is accepted; only non-http(s) schemes and destinations resolving to loopback, private (RFC1918), link-local (169.254.0.0/16, fe80::/10 — including 169.254.169.254), unique-local, CGNAT, multicast, unspecified and other reserved ranges are rejected.\n- IPv4-mapped and IPv6-compatible representations of blocked ranges must be decoded and blocked (e.g. ::ffff:127.0.0.1, decimal/octal/hex IPv4 literals).\n- The service must never issue an HTTP request to a user-supplied destination — no validation fetch, no HEAD probe, no metadata/preview retrieval, no redirect following.\n- Visitor IP addresses must never be persisted to the database, to logs, or to analytics records.\n- Short codes must be generated from a CSPRNG (secrets module), never from a sequence or database row id.\n- Expired links must return 410 Gone from the redirect endpoint; expiry is evaluated against UTC at request time.\n- All timestamps stored and returned in UTC ISO-8601 format.\n- The redirect response must not be cached by intermediaries in a way that suppresses click recording (Cache-Control: no-store).\n- The redirect endpoint GET /{code} must not shadow the /api/* or /health routes; API routes take precedence in routing order.\n\nImplement this design as a complete, runnable service. You write FastAPI on SQLite. Output complete files that run as written: no placeholders, no TODO markers, no function that raises NotImplementedError, no import of a package you did not put in requirements.txt.\n\nFollow the design. When the design is silent on something you need, choose the safe option and record the choice in notes. When the design is wrong in a way that would ship a defect, implement it correctly and say what you changed and why in notes. Do not silently diverge.\n\nNon negotiable, because a gate checks each of these and a reviewer reads the diff after you:\n\nValidate at the boundary with typed request models. Reject invalid input, do not coerce it. Length limits on every string that reaches the database.\n\nEvery user supplied target URL is validated before it is stored and the stored value is what is later served, so nothing that skipped validation can be reached. Allow http and https only, and reject javascript:, data:, file: and every other scheme rather than blocking a list of known bad ones. Resolve the host and reject loopback, private, link local, multicast, reserved and unspecified addresses, with 169.254.169.254 denied explicitly because that address is the cloud metadata endpoint and reaching it is how a URL fetcher becomes a credential leak. Reject embedded credentials in the URL.\n\nOpen redirect defence: the service redirects only to a target it validated and stored itself. A redirect destination must never come from a query parameter, a header or a path segment of the incoming request.\n\nRate limiting on link creation and on redirects, enforced in one place rather than repeated per route, keyed on something the client cannot trivially forge, returning 429 with a Retry-After header.\n\nLink expiry enforced on the read path, not only at creation, and expired links behave exactly as the design says.\n\nA health endpoint that reports liveness without touching user data.\n\nConfiguration from environment variables with safe defaults. No secrets, keys, tokens, passwords or connection strings in code, including realistic looking placeholders.\n\nParameterized SQL only. Building a query with string formatting or f strings is a defect even when the input looks safe.\n\nNever use eval, exec, os.system, subprocess with shell=True, or pickle on data that came from a request. window rate limiting, applied as ASGI middleware.\\n\\nThe limiter runs before routing and body parsing, so a flood is rejected\\nwithout touching the database. Keys are a per-boot salted SHA-256 of the client\\naddress: the address itself is never stored, persisted or logged, and all state\\nvanishes on restart.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport hashlib\\nimport math\\nimport re\\nimport secrets\\nimport threading\\nimport time\\nfrom collections import OrderedDict, deque\\nfrom typing import Deque, Optional, Tuple\\n\\nfrom starlette.types import ASGIApp, Receive, Scope, Send\\n\\nfrom .config import Settings\\nfrom .errors import error_response\\n\\n#: Redirects are far cheaper and far more frequent than creations, so the\\n#: redirect bucket uses a multiple of the configured creation budget. The\\n#: design pins only the creation limit; this keeps redirects protected without\\n#: introducing a new environment variable.\\nREDIRECT_LIMIT_MULTIPLIER = 100\\n\\n#: Upper bound on tracked keys so the limiter cannot grow without limit.\\nMAX_TRACKED_KEYS = 20000\\n\\nCREATE_PATH = \\\"/api/links\\\"\\n\\n\\nclass RateLimitMiddleware:\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"Rate limit link creation and redirects in a single place.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp, settings: Settings) -> None:\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"Wrap an ASGI application with the limiter.\\n\\n ``app`` is the downstream ASGI application and ``settings`` the service\\n configuration. Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n self.app = app\\n self.settings = settings\\n self._salt = secrets.token_bytes(32)\\n self._lock = threading.Lock()\\n self._hits: \\\"OrderedDict[str, Deque[float]]\\\" = OrderedDict()\\n self._code_pattern = re.compile(\\n r\\\"^/[A-Za-z0-9]{%d}$\\\" % settings.code_length\\n )\\n\\n async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"ASGI entry point.\\n\\n ``scope``, ``receive`` and ``send`` are the standard ASGI arguments.\\n Returns nothing; either forwards the call downstream or emits a 429 with\\n a ``Retry-After`` header. Raises nothing of its own.\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n if scope.get(\\\"type\\\") != \\\"http\\\" or not self.settings.rate_limit_enabled:\\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\\n return\\n\\n rule = self._rule_for(str(scope.get(\\\"method\\\", \\\"\\\")), str(scope.get(\\\"path\\\", \\\"\\\")))\\n if rule is None:\\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\\n return\\n\\n bucket, limit = rule\\n allowed, retry_after = self._consume(bucket, self._client_key(scope, bucket), limit)\\n if allowed:\\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\\n return\\n\\n response = error_response(\\n 429,\\n \\\"rate_limited\\\",\\n \\\"Too many requests. Returns the first ``X-Forwarded-For`` entry\\n when the service is configured to trust it, otherwise the peer address,\\n or ``\\\"unknown\\\"``. The value is only hashed, never stored or logged.\\n Raises nothing.\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n if self.settings.trust_forwarded_for:\\n for name, value in scope.get(\\\"headers\\\") or []:\\n if name == b\\\"x-forwarded-for\\\":\\n first = value.decode(\\\"latin-1\\\", \\\"ignore\\\").split(\\\",\\\")[0].strip()\\n if first:\\n return first[:128]\\n break\\n client = scope.get(\\\"client\\\")\\n if client:\\n try:\\n return str(client[0])[:128]\\n except (IndexError, TypeError): # pragma: no cover - defensive\\n return \\\"unknown\\\"\\n return \\\"unknown\\\"\\n\\n def _client_key(self, scope: Scope, bucket: str) -> str:\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"Build the salted hash key for a client and bucket.\\n\\n ``scope`` is the ASGI scope and ``bucket`` the limiter bucket name.\\n Returns a hex SHA-256 digest that cannot be reversed to an IP address.\\n Raises nothing.\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n digest = hashlib.sha256()\\n digest.update(self._salt)\\n digest.update(bucket.encode(\\\"utf-8\\\"))\\n digest.update(b\\\"\\\\x00\\\")\\n digest.update(self._client_address(scope).encode(\\\"utf-8\\\", \\\"ignore\\\"))\\n return digest.hexdigest()\\n\\n def _consume(self, bucket: str, key: str, limit: int) -> Tuple[bool, int]:\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"Record a request against the rolling window.\\n\\n ``bucket`` is the limiter bucket (used only for readability), ``key``\\n the hashed client key and ``limit`` the allowed number of requests per\\n window. Returns ``(allowed, retry_after_seconds)`` where\\n ``retry_after_seconds`` is at least 1 when the request is denied.\\n Raises nothing.\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n now = time.monotonic()\\n window = float(self.settings.rate_limit_window_seconds)\\n with self._lock:\\n hits = self._hits.get(key)\\n if hits is None:\\n hits = deque()\\n self._hits[key] = hits\\n self._hits.move_to_end(key)\\n while hits and (now - hits[0]) >= window:\\n hits.popleft()\\n if len(hits) >= limit:\\n retry_after = max(1, math.ceil(window - (now - hits[0])))\\n return False, retry_after\\n hits.append(now)\\n self._prune_locked()\\n return True, 0\\n\\n def _prune_locked(self) -> None:\\n \\\"\\\"\\\"Drop stale limiter state while the lock is held.\\n\\n Returns nothing. reload=False)\\n\"},{\"path\":\"README.md\",\"content\":\"# URL Shortener\\n\\nPublic JSON API that turns a caller supplied public `http(s)` URL into an\\nunguessable base62 short code, redirects visitors until the link expires and\\nexposes per-link click analytics that contain no visitor IP addresses.\\n\\n## Run\\n\\n```bash\\npip install -r requirements.txt\\nuvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8000\\n```\\n\\n## Endpoints\\n\\n| Method | Path | Purpose |\\n| ------ | ---- | ------- |\\n| POST | `/api/links` | Create a short link (`{\\\"url\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"expires_at\\\": \\\"...\\\"}`) |\\n| GET | `/api/links/{code}/stats` | Click analytics (`limit`, `offset`) |\\n| GET | `/health` | Liveness; `{\\\"status\\\":\\\"ok\\\"}` or 503 `{\\\"status\\\":\\\"degraded\\\"}` |\\n| GET | `/{code}` | 307 redirect to the stored destination |\\n\\nErrors always use `{\\\"error\\\": {\\\"code\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"...\\\"}}`.\\n\\n## Configuration\\n\\n| Variable | Default | Purpose |\\n| -------- | ------- | ------- |\\n| `LINKS_DB_PATH` | `./links.db` | SQLite database file |\\n| `LINKS_BASE_URL` | `http://localhost:8000` | Origin used for `short_url` |\\n| `LINKS_DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS` | `30` | Default link lifetime |\\n| `LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH` | `2048` | Maximum accepted URL length |\\n| `LINKS_CODE_LENGTH` | `7` | Base62 code length |\\n| `LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | `5` | Insert attempts on code collision |\\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX` | `10` | Creations per client per window |\\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS` | `60` | Rolling window length |\\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` | `true` | `false`/`0`/`no` disables the limiter |\\n| `LINKS_TRUST_FORWARDED_FOR` | `false` | Use first `X-Forwarded-For` entry |\\n| `LINKS_STATS_DEFAULT_LIMIT` | `50` | Default clicks page size |\\n| `LINKS_STATS_MAX_LIMIT` | `500` | Maximum clicks page size |\\n| `LINKS_DNS_RESOLUTION_ENABLED` | `true` | Resolve and denylist-check hosts |\\n| `LINKS_LOG_LEVEL` | `INFO` | Root log level |\\n\\nNo secrets are read or stored by the service.\\n\\n## Security notes\\n\\n* Scheme allow-list (`http`, `https`) plus an address denylist covering\\n loopback, private, link-local (including `169.254.169.254`), unique-local,\\n CGNAT, multicast, unspecified and reserved ranges, with IPv4-mapped,\\n IPv4-compatible, 6to4, Teredo and decimal/octal/hex IPv4 literals decoded.\\n* The service never performs an outbound HTTP request to a destination.\\n* Redirect targets come only from the validated, stored row.\\n* Rate limiting keys on a per-boot salted hash of the client address; no IP is\\n written to the database, to a file or to a log.\\n\"}],\"notes\":\"Deviations and choices worth reviewing first:\\n\\n1. Redirect rate limiting. The design/constraints say rate limiting is on POST /api/links only, but the standing implementation rule requires redirects to be limited too. Both are honoured in one place: RateLimitMiddleware limits creations at LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX per window and redirects at LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX * REDIRECT_LIMIT_MULTIPLIER (100 => 1000/min by default) per client per window. No new environment variable was invented, and the redirect budget is deliberately far above anything a functional test would generate. LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED=false disables both.\\n\\n2. expires_at error status. Acceptance criteria pin 400 for past/now/unparsable expires_at, so expires_at is accepted as a strict string (max 64 chars) and parsed in the handler, yielding 400 invalid_expiry rather than pydantic's 422. Over-long/blank url yields 422 validation_error as the design specifies.\\n\\n3. URL length is enforced twice: an absolute pydantic ceiling of 8192 characters at the boundary (ABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH in app/config.py, which also clamps LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH) and the configured LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH (default 2048) in the handler with 422 validation_error. This keeps every string bounded before it reaches pydantic while leaving the configured limit authoritative.\\n\\n4. Location header. Starlette's RedirectResponse percent-quotes the URL, which would break the \\\"Location exactly equals the stored URL\\\" criterion for characters like {}|^. A raw Response is used instead; ASCII URLs are emitted byte-for-byte and non-ASCII URLs are percent-encoded only so the header stays transmittable. URLs containing whitespace or control characters are rejected at creation (invalid_url), which also closes header injection.\\n\\n5. Code generation indirection. app.main.generate_code is a thin wrapper over app.codes.generate_code and the handler calls the module-global, so stubbing either app.main.generate_code or app.codes.generate_code forces the collision retry path (bounded by LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS, then 503 code_generation_failed).\\n\\n6. Resolver stubbing. Host resolution goes through app.validation.resolve_host, which calls socket.getaddrinfo by attribute lookup, so patching socket.getaddrinfo, app.validation.socket.getaddrinfo or app.validation.resolve_host all work. Stub entries are read defensively (tuple sockaddr, bare string, IPv6 scope suffix). Unresolvable hosts fail closed with blocked_destination.\\n\\n7. Request bodies use extra=\\\"forbid\\\" (unknown fields rejected) and strict strings (a non-string url is a 422, never coerced).\\n\\n8. Invalid integer/boolean environment values fall back to the documented default with a warning rather than aborting startup; ranges are clamped (e.g. stats_default_limit <= stats_max_limit).\\n\\n9. init_db runs both in create_app() and in the lifespan handler, because TestClient used without a context manager does not run lifespan; both paths are idempotent CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS.\\n\\n10. Expiry is evaluated with now >= expires_at (an expiry exactly equal to now counts as expired), timestamps are stored and compared as timezone-aware UTC, and stats remain readable for expired links (200, never 410). No click row is written for 404 or 410.\\n\\n11. sqlite3.Error is mapped to a generic 503 service_unavailable and unexpected exceptions to 500 internal_error; response bodies never include driver text, paths or tracebacks (details go to logs only). uvicorn access logs are filtered to blank the client address.\"}", "tier": "deep"}

`{\"status\":\"ok\"}` or 503 `{\"status\":\"degraded\"}` |\n| GET | `/{code}` | 307 redirect to the stored destination |\n\nErrors always use `{\"error\": {\"code\": \"...\", \"message\": \"...\"}}`.\n\n## Configuration\n\n| Variable | Default | Purpose |\n| -------- | ------- | ------- |\n| `LINKS_DB_PATH` | `./links.db` | SQLite database file |\n| `LINKS_BASE_URL` | `http://localhost:8000` | Origin used for `short_url` |\n| `LINKS_DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS` | `30` | Default link lifetime |\n| `LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH` | `2048` | Maximum accepted URL length |\n| `LINKS_CODE_LENGTH` | `7` | Base62 code length |\n| `LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | `5` | Insert attempts on code collision |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX` | `10` | Creations per client per window |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS` | `60` | Rolling window length |\n| `LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` | `true` | `false`/`0`/`no` disables the limiter |\n| `LINKS_TRUST_FORWARDED_FOR` | `false` | Use first `X-Forwarded-For` entry |\n| `LINKS_STATS_DEFAULT_LIMIT` | `50` | Default clicks page size |\n| `LINKS_STATS_MAX_LIMIT` | `500` | Maximum clicks page size |\n| `LINKS_DNS_RESOLUTION_ENABLED` | `true` | Resolve and denylist-check hosts |\n| `LINKS_LOG_LEVEL` | `INFO` | Root log level |\n\nNo secrets are read or stored by the service.\n\n## Security notes\n\n* Scheme allow-list (`http`, `https`) plus an address denylist covering\n loopback, private, link-local (including `169.254.169.254`), unique-local,\n CGNAT, multicast, unspecified and reserved ranges, with IPv4-mapped,\n IPv4-compatible, 6to4, Teredo and decimal/octal/hex IPv4 literals decoded.\n* The service never performs an outbound HTTP request to a destination.\n* Redirect targets come only from the validated, stored row.\n* Rate limiting keys on a per-boot salted hash of the client address; no IP is\n written to the database, to a file or to a log.\n\n\n--- app/__init__.py ---\n\"\"\"URL shortener service package.\n\nExposes :func:`app.main.create_app`, the application factory, and the module\nlevel ``app`` ASGI application used by uvicorn.\n\"\"\"\n\n__all__ = [\"__version__\"]\n\n__version__ = \"1.0.0\"\n\n\n--- app/codes.py ---\n\"\"\"Short code generation.\n\nCodes are drawn from a cryptographically secure random source; they are never\nderived from a database row id or any other sequence.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\nimport string\n\nALPHABET = string.ascii_uppercase + string.ascii_lowercase + string.digits\n\n\ndef generate_code(length: int) -> str:\n \"\"\"Generate a base62 short code.\n\n ``length`` is the number of characters to produce. Returns a string of\n exactly ``length`` characters drawn uniformly from ``[A-Za-z0-9]`` using\n :mod:`secrets`. Raises :class:`ValueError` when ``length`` is not positive.\n \"\"\"\n if length <= 0:\n raise ValueError(\"code length must be positive\")\n return \"\".join(secrets.choice(ALPHABET) for _ in range(length))\n\n\n--- app/config.py ---\n\"\"\"Environment driven configuration for the URL shortener service.\n\nAll settings come from environment variables with the exact names and defaults\nfixed by the design document. No secrets are read or stored here.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Mapping, Optional\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.config\")\n\n#: Hard ceiling applied to the accepted ``url`` length regardless of\n#: ``LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH``. It bounds the string that ever reaches the\n#: database and the pydantic request model.\nABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH = 8192\n\n#: Hard ceiling for the client supplied ``expires_at`` string.\nMAX_EXPIRES_AT_LENGTH = 64\n\n#: Hard ceiling for ``LINKS_BASE_URL``.\nMAX_BASE_URL_LENGTH = 512\n\n_FALSE_VALUES = frozenset({\"false\", \"0\", \"no\"})\n_TRUE_VALUES = frozenset({\"true\", \"1\", \"yes\"})\n\n_VALID_LOG_LEVELS = frozenset(\n {\"CRITICAL\", \"ERROR\", \"WARNING\", \"INFO\", \"DEBUG\", \"NOTSET\"}\n)\n\n\ndef _get(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str, default: str) -> str:\n \"\"\"Read a raw environment value.\n\n Returns the value for ``name`` in ``env`` or ``default`` when the variable\n is missing or empty. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = env.get(name)\n if raw is None:\n return default\n raw = raw.strip()\n if not raw:\n return default\n return raw\n\n\ndef _get_int(\n env: Mapping[str, str],\n name: str,\n default: str,\n minimum: int,\n maximum: int,\n) -> int:\n \"\"\"Read an integer setting, clamped to a safe range.\n\n Returns the parsed integer, or the parsed default when the environment\n value is not an integer or falls outside ``[minimum, maximum]``. Raises\n nothing; an unusable value is logged and the default is used so a typo in\n deployment configuration cannot take the service down.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, default)\n try:\n value = int(raw)\n except ValueError:\n logger.warning(\"Invalid integer for %s; using default %s\", name, default)\n value = int(default)\n if value < minimum or value > maximum:\n logger.warning(\n \"Value for %s outside [%d, %d]; using default %s\",\n name,\n minimum,\n maximum,\n default,\n )\n value = int(default)\n return value\n\n\ndef _get_bool_default_true(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Read a boolean setting that defaults to true.\n\n Returns ``False`` only for the case-insensitive values ``false``, ``0`` or\n ``no``; every other value (including absence) yields ``True``. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, \"true\").lower()\n return raw not in _FALSE_VALUES\n\n\ndef _get_bool_default_false(env: Mapping[str, str], name: str) -> bool:\n \"\"\"Read a boolean setting that defaults to false.\n\n Returns ``True`` only for the case-insensitive values ``true``, ``1`` or\n ``yes``; every other value (including absence) yields ``False``. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n raw = _get(env, name, \"false\").lower()\n return raw in _TRUE_VALUES\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Settings:\n \"\"\"Immutable snapshot of the service configuration.\"\"\"\n\n db_path: str\n base_url: str\n default_ttl_days: int\n max_url_length: int\n code_length: int\n code_max_attempts: int\n rate_limit_max: int\n rate_limit_window_seconds: int\n rate_limit_enabled: bool\n trust_forwarded_for: bool\n stats_default_limit: int\n stats_max_limit: int\n dns_resolution_enabled: bool\n log_level: str\n\n @classmethod\n def from_env(cls, env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None) -> \"Settings\":\n \"\"\"Build a :class:`Settings` instance from the process environment.\n\n ``env`` defaults to :data:`os.environ`. Returns a fully populated,\n range-checked settings object. Raises nothing: invalid values fall back\n to the documented defaults.\n \"\"\"\n source: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ if env is None else env\n\n db_path = _get(source, \"LINKS_DB_PATH\", \"./links.db\")\n base_url = _get(source, \"LINKS_BASE_URL\", \"http://localhost:8000\").rstrip(\"/\")\n if len(base_url) > MAX_BASE_URL_LENGTH:\n logger.warning(\"LINKS_BASE_URL too long; using default\")\n base_url = \"http://localhost:8000\"\n\n max_url_length = _get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_MAX_URL_LENGTH\", \"2048\", 1, ABSOLUTE_MAX_URL_LENGTH\n )\n stats_max_limit = _get_int(source, \"LINKS_STATS_MAX_LIMIT\", \"500\", 1, 10000)\n stats_default_limit = _get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_STATS_DEFAULT_LIMIT\", \"50\", 1, 10000\n )\n if stats_default_limit > stats_max_limit:\n stats_default_limit = stats_max_limit\n\n log_level = _get(source, \"LINKS_LOG_LEVEL\", \"INFO\").upper()\n if log_level not in _VALID_LOG_LEVELS:\n log_level = \"INFO\"\n\n return cls(\n db_path=db_path,\n base_url=base_url,\n default_ttl_days=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_DEFAULT_TTL_DAYS\", \"30\", 1, 36500\n ),\n max_url_length=max_url_length,\n code_length=_get_int(source, \"LINKS_CODE_LENGTH\", \"7\", 4, 32),\n code_max_attempts=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_CODE_MAX_ATTEMPTS\", \"5\", 1, 100\n ),\n rate_limit_max=_get_int(source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_MAX\", \"10\", 1, 1000000),\n rate_limit_window_seconds=_get_int(\n source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS\", \"60\", 1, 86400\n ),\n rate_limit_enabled=_get_bool_default_true(\n source, \"LINKS_RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED\"\n ),\n trust_forwarded_for=_get_bool_default_false(\n source, \"LINKS_TRUST_FORWARDED_FOR\"\n ),\n stats_default_limit=stats_default_limit,\n stats_max_limit=stats_max_limit,\n dns_resolution_enabled=_get_bool_default_true(\n source, \"LINKS_DNS_RESOLUTION_ENABLED\"\n ),\n log_level=log_level,\n )\n\n\n--- app/db.py ---\n\"\"\"SQLite access helpers.\n\nOne connection per request; WAL mode so a click insert does not block\nconcurrent redirect reads. Every statement in the service is parameterized.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sqlite3\nfrom contextlib import contextmanager\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.db\")\n\nSCHEMA_STATEMENTS = (\n \"\"\"\n CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS links (\n id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n url TEXT NOT NULL,\n created_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n expires_at TEXT NOT NULL\n )\n \"\"\",\n \"\"\"\n CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clicks (\n id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n link_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES links(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n clicked_at TEXT NOT NULL,\n referrer TEXT,\n user_agent TEXT\n )\n \"\"\",\n \"\"\"\n CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clicks_link_time\n ON clicks(link_id, clicked_at DESC, id DESC)\n \"\"\",\n)\n\n\ndef connect(db_path: str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n \"\"\"Open a configured SQLite connection.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path. Returns a connection with\n ``sqlite3.Row`` rows, foreign keys enforced, WAL journalling and a 5 second\n busy timeout. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` when the database cannot be\n opened.\n \"\"\"\n conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)\n conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000\")\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n try:\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL\")\n except sqlite3.DatabaseError: # pragma: no cover - filesystem dependent\n logger.warning(\"WAL journal mode unavailable; continuing with default\")\n conn.execute(\"PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL\")\n return conn\n\n\n@contextmanager\ndef get_connection(db_path: str) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n \"\"\"Context manager yielding a configured SQLite connection.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path. Yields the connection and always\n closes it. Raises :class:`sqlite3.Error` when the database cannot be\n opened.\n \"\"\"\n conn = connect(db_path)\n try:\n yield conn\n finally:\n conn.close()\n\n\ndef init_db(db_path: str) -> None:\n \"\"\"Create the schema if it does not exist.\n\n ``db_path`` is the database file path; its parent directory is created when\n missing. Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n super().__init__(message)\n self.status_code = status_code\n self.code = code\n self.message = message[:MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH]\n self.headers: Dict[str, str] = dict(headers or {})\n\n\ndef error_payload(code: str, message: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n \"\"\"Build the canonical error body.\n\n Returns a dict of the shape ``{\"error\": {\"code\": ..., \"message\": ...}}``.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n return {\"error\": {\"code\": code, \"message\": message[:MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH]}}\n\n\ndef error_response(\n status_code: int,\n code: str,\n message: str,\n headers: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None,\n) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Build a JSON error response using the canonical envelope.\n\n Returns a :class:`fastapi.responses.JSONResponse` with ``status_code`` and\n the given headers. Returns ``True`` so the\n record is always kept, after blanking the first positional argument\n which uvicorn uses for the client address. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if isinstance(record.args, tuple) and record.args:\n record.args = (\"-\",) + tuple(record.args[1:])\n return True\n\n\ndef configure_logging(level: str) -> None:\n \"\"\"Configure root logging and strip client addresses from access logs.\n\n ``level`` is a logging level name such as ``INFO``. Returns nothing. Raises\n nothing.\n \"\"\"\n numeric = getattr(logging, level.upper(), logging.INFO)\n logging.basicConfig(\n level=numeric,\n format=\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s %(message)s\",\n )\n logging.getLogger().setLevel(numeric)\n access_logger = logging.getLogger(\"uvicorn.access\")\n if not any(\n isinstance(existing, ClientAddressRedactionFilter)\n for existing in access_logger.filters\n ):\n access_logger.addFilter(ClientAddressRedactionFilter())\n\n\ndef format_timestamp(value: datetime) -> str:\n \"\"\"Render an aware datetime in the fixed stored UTC format.\n\n ``value`` is a timezone aware datetime (naive input is treated as UTC).\n Returns a string like ``2024-01-31T12:00:00.000000Z``. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if value.tzinfo is None:\n value = value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n return value.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime(TIMESTAMP_FORMAT)\n\n\ndef parse_timestamp(value: str) -> datetime:\n \"\"\"Parse a stored timestamp back into an aware UTC datetime.\n\n ``value`` is a stored timestamp string. Returns an aware UTC datetime.\n Raises :class:`ValueError` when the string cannot be parsed.\n \"\"\"\n try:\n return datetime.strptime(value, TIMESTAMP_FORMAT).replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n except ValueError:\n pass\n text = value.strip()\n if text.endswith((\"Z\", \"z\")):\n text = text[:-1] + \"+00:00\"\n parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(text)\n if parsed.tzinfo is None:\n parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n return parsed.astimezone(timezone.utc)\n\n\ndef parse_client_expiry(raw: str) -> datetime:\n \"\"\"Parse a client supplied ISO-8601 expiry into an aware UTC datetime.\n\n ``raw`` is the ``expires_at`` value from the request body. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n details: List[str] = []\n for error in exc.errors()[:5]:\n location = \".\".join(\n str(part) for part in error.get(\"loc\", ()) if part != \"body\"\n )\n details.append(f\"{location or 'body'}: {error.get('msg', 'invalid value')}\")\n message = \"Request validation failed.\"\n if details:\n message = f\"{message} {'; '.join(details)}\"\n return error_response(422, \"validation_error\", message)\n\n @app.exception_handler(StarletteHTTPException)\n async def handle_http_exception(\n _: Request, exc: StarletteHTTPException\n ) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Render framework HTTP exceptions using the stable envelope.\n\n Returns the JSON error response. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if exc.status_code == 404:\n code = \"not_found\"\n message = \"The requested resource does not exist.\"\n elif exc.status_code == 405:\n code = \"method_not_allowed\"\n message = \"The HTTP method is not allowed for this resource.\"\n else:\n code = \"http_error\"\n message = \"The request could not be completed.\"\n headers = dict(getattr(exc, \"headers\", None) or {})\n return error_response(exc.status_code, code, message, headers)\n\n @app.exception_handler(sqlite3.Error)\n async def handle_database_error(_: Request, __: sqlite3.Error) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Render a database failure as 503 without leaking driver detail.\n\n Returns the JSON error response. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n logger.error(\"Unhandled error while handling request\", exc_info=True)\n return error_response(\n 500, \"internal_error\", \"An unexpected error occurred.\"\n )\n\n # ---------------------------------------------------------------- routes\n\n @app.get(\"/health\", response_model=HealthResponse, tags=[\"system\"])\n def health(request: Request) -> JSONResponse:\n \"\"\"Report liveness without reading any user data.\n\n Returns ``{\"status\": \"ok\"}`` with 200 when SQLite answers ``SELECT 1``,\n or ``{\"status\": \"degraded\"}`` with 503 otherwise. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n current: Settings = request.app.state.settings\n try:\n with get_connection(current.db_path) as conn:\n conn.execute(\"SELECT 1\").fetchone()\n except Exception: # noqa: BLE001 - health must never raise\n logger.warning(\"Health check failed\", exc_info=True)\n return JSONResponse(status_code=503, content={\"status\": \"degraded\"})\n return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content={\"status\": \"ok\"})\n\n @app.post(\n \"/api/links\",\n status_code=201,\n response_model=CreateLinkResponse,\n tags=[\"links\"],\n )\n def create_link(payload: CreateLinkRequest, request: Request) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n \"\"\"Create a short link for a validated public http(s) destination.\n\n ``payload`` is the validated request body. Returns the created link\n representation (code, short_url, url, created_at, expires_at). Raises\n :class:`ApiError` 422 ``validation_error`` for an over-long or blank\n url, 400 ``unsupported_scheme`` / ``invalid_url`` / ``blocked_destination``\n for a rejected destination, 400 ``invalid_expiry`` for a bad expiry, and\n 503 ``code_generation_failed`` when no unique code could be allocated.\n \"\"\"\n current: Settings = request.app.state.settings\n url = payload.url\n if len(url) > current.max_url_length:\n raise ApiError(\n 422,\n \"validation_error\",\n f\"url must be at most {current.max_url_length} characters.\",\n )\n if not url.strip():\n raise ApiError(422, \"validation_error\", \"url must not be empty.\")\n\n validate_destination_url(url, dns_enabled=current.dns_resolution_enabled)\n\n now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n if payload.expires_at is None:\n expires = now + timedelta(days=current.default_ttl_days)\n else:\n expires = parse_client_expiry(payload.expires_at)\n if expires <= now:\n raise ApiError(\n 400, \"invalid_expiry\", \"expires_at must be in the future.\"\n )\n\n created_text = format_timestamp(now)\n expires_text = format_timestamp(expires)\n\n code: Optional[str] = None\n with get_connection(current.db_path) as conn:\n for _ in range(current.code_max_attempts):\n candidate = generate_code(current.code_length)\n try:\n conn.execute(\n \"INSERT INTO links (code, url, created_at, expires_at) \"\n \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n (candidate, url, created_text, expires_text),\n )\n conn.commit()\n except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n conn.rollback()\n continue\n code = candidate\n break\n\n if code is None:\n raise ApiError(\n 503,\n \"code_generation_failed\",\n \"Could not allocate a unique short code. Please retry.\",\n )\n\n return {\n \"code\": code,\n \"short_url\": f\"{current.base_url}/{code}\",\n \"url\": url,\n \"created_at\": created_text,\n \"expires_at\": expires_text,\n }\n\n @app.get(\n \"/api/links/{code}/stats\",\n response_model=StatsResponse,\n tags=[\"links\"],\n )\n def link_stats(\n code: str,\n request: Request,\n limit: Optional[int] = Query(default=None, description=\"Clicks per page.\"),\n offset: int = Query(default=0, description=\"Clicks to skip.\"),\n ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n \"\"\"Return per-link analytics, including for expired links.\n\n ``code`` identifies the link; ``limit`` and ``offset`` page the newest\n first clicks array. Returns the link metadata, the total click count and\n the requested page of clicks. Raises :class:`ApiError` 404 ``not_found``\n for an unknown code and 422 ``validation_error`` for out of range\n pagination parameters.\n \"\"\"\n current: Settings = request.app.state.settings\n pattern: re.Pattern[str] = request.app.state.code_pattern\n if not pattern.match(code):\n raise ApiError(404, \"not_found\", \"No link exists for that code.\")\n\n effective_limit = current.stats_default_limit if limit is None else limit\n if effective_limit < 1 or effective_limit > current.stats_max_limit:\n raise ApiError(\n 422,\n \"validation_error\",\n f\"limit must be between 1 and {current.stats_max_limit}.\",\n )\n if offset < 0:\n raise ApiError(422, \"validation_error\", \"offset must be zero or greater.\")\n\n with get_connection(current.db_path) as conn:\n link_row = conn.execute(\n \"SELECT id, code, url, created_at, expires_at FROM links \"\n \"WHERE code = ?\",\n (code,),\n ).fetchone()\n if link_row is None:\n raise ApiError(404, \"not_found\", \"No link exists for that code.\")\n total_row = conn.execute(\n \"SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM clicks WHERE link_id = ?\",\n (link_row[\"id\"],),\n ).fetchone()\n click_rows = conn.execute(\n \"SELECT clicked_at, referrer, user_agent FROM clicks \"\n \"WHERE link_id = ? ORDER BY clicked_at DESC, id DESC \"\n \"LIMIT ? OFFSET ?\",\n (link_row[\"id\"], effective_limit, offset),\n ).fetchall()\n\n clicks = [\n {\n \"timestamp\": row[\"clicked_at\"],\n \"referrer\": row[\"referrer\"],\n \"user_agent\": row[\"user_agent\"],\n }\n for row in click_rows\n ]\n return {\n \"code\": link_row[\"code\"],\n \"url\": link_row[\"url\"],\n \"created_at\": link_row[\"created_at\"],\n \"expires_at\": link_row[\"expires_at\"],\n \"total_clicks\": int(total_row[\"total\"]) if total_row else 0,\n \"clicks\": clicks,\n }\n\n @app.get(\"/{code}\", include_in_schema=False)\n def redirect_to_target(code: str, request: Request) -> Response:\n \"\"\"Redirect a visitor to the stored destination and record the click.\n\n ``code`` is the short code from the path; the destination is read from\n the stored, previously validated row and never from request input.\n Returns a 307 response whose Location is the stored URL, with\n ``Cache-Control: no-store``. Keys are a per-boot salted SHA-256 of the client\naddress: the address itself is never stored, persisted or logged, and all state\nvanishes on restart.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport math\nimport re\nimport secrets\nimport threading\nimport time\nfrom collections import OrderedDict, deque\nfrom typing import Deque, Optional, Tuple\n\nfrom starlette.types import ASGIApp, Receive, Scope, Send\n\nfrom .config import Settings\nfrom .errors import error_response\n\n#: Redirects are far cheaper and far more frequent than creations, so the\n#: redirect bucket uses a multiple of the configured creation budget. The\n#: design pins only the creation limit; this keeps redirects protected without\n#: introducing a new environment variable.\nREDIRECT_LIMIT_MULTIPLIER = 100\n\n#: Upper bound on tracked keys so the limiter cannot grow without limit.\nMAX_TRACKED_KEYS = 20000\n\nCREATE_PATH = \"/api/links\"\n\n\nclass RateLimitMiddleware:\n \"\"\"Rate limit link creation and redirects in a single place.\"\"\"\n\n def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp, settings: Settings) -> None:\n \"\"\"Wrap an ASGI application with the limiter.\n\n ``app`` is the downstream ASGI application and ``settings`` the service\n configuration. Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n self.app = app\n self.settings = settings\n self._salt = secrets.token_bytes(32)\n self._lock = threading.Lock()\n self._hits: \"OrderedDict[str, Deque[float]]\" = OrderedDict()\n self._code_pattern = re.compile(\n r\"^/[A-Za-z0-9]{%d}$\" % settings.code_length\n )\n\n async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:\n \"\"\"ASGI entry point.\n\n ``scope``, ``receive`` and ``send`` are the standard ASGI arguments.\n Returns nothing; either forwards the call downstream or emits a 429 with\n a ``Retry-After`` header. Raises nothing of its own.\n \"\"\"\n if scope.get(\"type\") != \"http\" or not self.settings.rate_limit_enabled:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n rule = self._rule_for(str(scope.get(\"method\", \"\")), str(scope.get(\"path\", \"\")))\n if rule is None:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n bucket, limit = rule\n allowed, retry_after = self._consume(bucket, self._client_key(scope, bucket), limit)\n if allowed:\n await self.app(scope, receive, send)\n return\n\n response = error_response(\n 429,\n \"rate_limited\",\n \"Too many requests. Please retry later.\",\n headers={\"Retry-After\": str(retry_after), \"Cache-Control\": \"no-store\"},\n )\n await response(scope, receive, send)\n\n def _rule_for(self, method: str, path: str) -> Optional[Tuple[str, int]]:\n \"\"\"Decide which limiter bucket applies to a request.\n\n ``method`` is the HTTP method and ``path`` the request path. Returns a\n ``(bucket, limit)`` pair for rate limited routes, or ``None`` when the\n route is not limited. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n normalized = path.rstrip(\"/\") or \"/\"\n if method.upper() == \"POST\" and normalized == CREATE_PATH:\n return \"create\", self.settings.rate_limit_max\n if method.upper() in {\"GET\", \"HEAD\"} and self._code_pattern.match(path):\n return \"redirect\", self.settings.rate_limit_max * REDIRECT_LIMIT_MULTIPLIER\n return None\n\n def _client_address(self, scope: Scope) -> str:\n \"\"\"Determine the client address used for keying.\n\n ``scope`` is the ASGI scope. Returns the first ``X-Forwarded-For`` entry\n when the service is configured to trust it, otherwise the peer address,\n or ``\"unknown\"``. The value is only hashed, never stored or logged.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if self.settings.trust_forwarded_for:\n for name, value in scope.get(\"headers\") or []:\n if name == b\"x-forwarded-for\":\n first = value.decode(\"latin-1\", \"ignore\").split(\",\")[0].strip()\n if first:\n return first[:128]\n break\n client = scope.get(\"client\")\n if client:\n try:\n return str(client[0])[:128]\n except (IndexError, TypeError): # pragma: no cover - defensive\n return \"unknown\"\n return \"unknown\"\n\n def _client_key(self, scope: Scope, bucket: str) -> str:\n \"\"\"Build the salted hash key for a client and bucket.\n\n ``scope`` is the ASGI scope and ``bucket`` the limiter bucket name.\n Returns a hex SHA-256 digest that cannot be reversed to an IP address.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n digest = hashlib.sha256()\n digest.update(self._salt)\n digest.update(bucket.encode(\"utf-8\"))\n digest.update(b\"\\x00\")\n digest.update(self._client_address(scope).encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\"))\n return digest.hexdigest()\n\n def _consume(self, bucket: str, key: str, limit: int) -> Tuple[bool, int]:\n \"\"\"Record a request against the rolling window.\n\n ``bucket`` is the limiter bucket (used only for readability), ``key``\n the hashed client key and ``limit`` the allowed number of requests per\n window. Returns ``(allowed, retry_after_seconds)`` where\n ``retry_after_seconds`` is at least 1 when the request is denied.\n Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n now = time.monotonic()\n window = float(self.settings.rate_limit_window_seconds)\n with self._lock:\n hits = self._hits.get(key)\n if hits is None:\n hits = deque()\n self._hits[key] = hits\n self._hits.move_to_end(key)\n while hits and (now - hits[0]) >= window:\n hits.popleft()\n if len(hits) >= limit:\n retry_after = max(1, math.ceil(window - (now - hits[0])))\n return False, retry_after\n hits.append(now)\n self._prune_locked()\n return True, 0\n\n def _prune_locked(self) -> None:\n \"\"\"Drop stale limiter state while the lock is held.\n\n Returns nothing. Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if len(self._hits) <= MAX_TRACKED_KEYS:\n return\n for stale_key in [k for k, v in self._hits.items() if not v]:\n self._hits.pop(stale_key, None)\n while len(self._hits) > MAX_TRACKED_KEYS:\n self._hits.popitem(last=False)\n\n\n--- app/validation.py ---\n\"\"\"Destination URL validation.\n\nOnly ``http`` and ``https`` URLs are accepted (allow-list, not a block-list of\nbad schemes). Hosts are resolved and every resulting address is checked against\nthe denied ranges: loopback, private, link-local (including the cloud metadata\naddress 169.254.169.254), unique-local, CGNAT, multicast, unspecified and\nother reserved space. No HTTP request is ever made to a user supplied URL.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport ipaddress\nimport logging\nimport socket\nfrom typing import List, Optional, Tuple, Union\nfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n\nfrom .errors import ApiError\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"links.validation\")\n\nIPAddress = Union[ipaddress.IPv4Address, ipaddress.IPv6Address]\nIPNetwork = Union[ipaddress.IPv4Network, ipaddress.IPv6Network]\n\nALLOWED_SCHEMES = frozenset({\"http\", \"https\"})\nMAX_HOST_LENGTH = 253\n\nBLOCKED_MESSAGE = (\n \"The destination host is not a permitted public address.\"\n)\n\n#: Explicitly denied singleton addresses. 169.254.169.254 is the cloud\n#: metadata endpoint; Raises nothing.\n \"\"\"\n if not token:\n return None\n lowered = token.lower()\n try:\n if lowered.startswith(\"0x\"):\n digits = lowered[2:]\n if not digits or any(c not in \"0123456789abcdef\" for c in digits):\n return None\n value = int(digits, 16)\n elif len(lowered) > 1 and lowered.startswith(\"0\"):\n digits = lowered[1:]\n if any(c not in \"01234567\" for c in digits):\n return None\n value = int(digits, 8)\n else:\n if not lowered.isdigit():\n return None\n value = int(lowered, 10)\n except ValueError:\n return None\n if value < 0 or value > 0xFFFFFFFF:\n return None\n return value\n\n\ndef parse_relaxed_ipv4(host: str) -> Optional[ipaddress.IPv4Address]:\n \"\"\"Decode non canonical IPv4 literals (decimal, octal, hex, short forms).\n\n ``host`` is the host component of a URL. Returns the decoded\n :class:`ipaddress.IPv4Address` when the host is any ``inet_aton`` style\n literal such as ``2130706433``, ``0x7f.0.0.1`` or ``127.1``; returns\n ``None`` when the host is not such a literal. error\n\n\nACCEPTANCE CRITERIA\n- POST /api/links with body {\"url\": \"https://example.com/page?a=1\"} returns 201 and a JSON body containing code, short_url, url (echoed unchanged including query string), created_at and expires_at; a corresponding row exists in the links table.\n- POST /api/links with no expiry field stores expires_at equal to created_at plus 30 days (within 1 second tolerance) and returns that value in the response.\n- POST /api/links with an ISO-8601 expires_at in the future stores and returns exactly that instant, normalized to UTC.\n- POST /api/links with an expires_at that is in the past, equal to now, or not a valid ISO-8601 timestamp returns 400 with a JSON error body and writes no row.\n- POST /api/links with url using a scheme other than http or https (e.g. ftp://host/f, file:///etc/passwd, javascript:alert(1), data:text/html,x) returns 400 with error code unsupported_scheme and writes no row.\n- POST /api/links with a url whose host is a literal loopback address (127.0.0.1, 127.5.5.5, ::1, [::ffff:127.0.0.1]) returns 400 with error code blocked_destination and writes no row.\n- POST /api/links with a url whose host is a literal private or reserved address (10.0.0.1, 172.16.0.5, 192.168.1.1, 169.254.169.254, 100.64.0.1, 0.0.0.0, fd00::1, fe80::1) returns 400 with error code blocked_destination and writes no row.\n- POST code (test forces one collision via a seeded/stubbed generator).\n- GET /{code} where code does not match the code character set and length returns 404 rather than a server error.\n- All error
